91:T2d1f,Bumps the pip group with 1 update in the /packages/training directory: [stanza](https://github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza).

Updates `stanza` from 1.10.1 to 1.12.2
<details>
<summary>Release notes</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/releases">stanza's releases</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2>v1.12.2 - weights_only security fix</h2>
<p>This is the same as v1.12.1, with the following important update: all model now enforce <code>weights_only=True</code> when loading.  This may obsolete some old models (all models distributed with Stanza are already patched).  If that happens, please load and then resave your model with an older version of Stanza, such as v1.12.1.</p>
<h2>Security / PyTorch compatibility</h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Enforce <code>weights_only=True</code> when loading the lemma classifier, addressing part of the security advisory <a href="https://github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/security/advisories/GHSA-v5jw-96jm-7h2c">GHSA-v5jw-96jm-7h2c</a>. This should already be the default in later versions of PyTorch, but is now explicitly enforced. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1584">#1584</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>All models now have the code path which allows for <code>weights_only=False</code> removed.  Instead, attempting to load a legacy model will throw an exception.  If that happens, please load your model with an older version (such as 1.12.1) and resave it before proceeding.  <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1587">#1587</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<h2>Tokenizer improvements</h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Add control characters to the set of characters treated as whitespace when tokenizing, fixing a bug where certain Unicode control characters (such as &quot;region end&quot; markers) were incorrectly attached to words. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1573">#1573</a> Addresses <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1257">#1257</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Add tokenizer augmentation that occasionally replaces commas with en-dashes or em-dashes, so that models trained on datasets that lack those characters learn to treat them similarly to commas. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1573">#1573</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Add regression tests for Spanish tokenization errors reported in <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1257">#1257</a> and tests for the whitespace/control-character handling and tokenizer augmentations. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1573">#1573</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<h2>Lemmatizer improvements</h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Enforce <code>weights_only=True</code> when loading the lemma classifier, avoiding a possible security risk. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1584">#1584</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>The lemma classifier for <code>ja_gsd</code> is now also attached to <code>ja_combined</code>. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1584">#1584</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Train and attach two lemma classifiers to <code>en_combined</code> — both <code>'s</code> and <code>her</code> can be reliably classified from the available data. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1584">#1584</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Add end-to-end unit tests for <code>run_lemma.py</code>, including training a lemmatizer and attaching multiple lemma classifiers. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1586">#1586</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<h2>Spanish model improvements</h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Add a silver dataset covering <code>como_VERB</code> in Spanish to the combined Spanish training data, addressing <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1440">#1440</a>. Also adds a utility to print a confusion matrix of tagging results filtered by a word regex (e.g. <code>--upos_word_regex &quot;^(?i:como)$&quot;</code>), making it easier to isolate the effects of annotation changes. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1579">#1579</a> <a href="https://github.com/stanfordnlp/handparsed-treebank/commit/d0c29a30659a77579d72b7a1c34744d9e8c2193b">stanfordnlp/handparsed-treebank@d0c29a3</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Add silver training sentences covering unknown Spanish VERB lemmas to the combined Spanish lemmatizer, addressing <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1255">#1255</a>. Also includes a script to check lemmatizer results for a batch of word/POS combinations. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1580">#1580</a> <a href="https://github.com/stanfordnlp/handparsed-treebank/commit/11327ef2dd83bc59ffbbe9497ade19629e9154ac">stanfordnlp/handparsed-treebank@11327ef</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
<h2>Italian model improvements</h2>
<ul>
<li>Rebuild Italian models with additional training data to fix incorrect lemmatization of common words including &quot;violino&quot; (was incorrectly mapped to &quot;violare&quot;) <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1563">#1563</a> <a href="https://github.com/stanfordnlp/handparsed-treebank/commit/9c46db103191699038f60eeb294f85ec23352b84">stanfordnlp/handparsed-treebank@9c46db1</a>, and &quot;diversi&quot; (was incorrectly split and mapped to &quot;dire&quot;) — resolved by retraining with the more accurate models <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1564">#1564</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>English model improvements</h2>
<ul>
<li>The long-standing issue of &quot;can&quot; being tagged as a modal verb (MD) rather than a noun (NN) in noun phrases like &quot;trash can&quot; and &quot;soda can&quot; is now resolved with the combined English models. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/408">#408</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>New / updated models</h2>
<ul>
<li>Odia (Oriya) now uses the ODTB package as the default. Mixed POS and depparse training data is constructed from the Odia dataset combined with related Indic languages present in MuRIL-Large, following the approach used for Sindhi. The Odia NER model is now also connected to the default package. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1583">#1583</a></li>
</ul>
<h2>Demo and visualization improvements</h2>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Rewrite <code>stanza-parseviewer.js</code> to use a proper constituency parse visualizer instead of a repurposed dependency parse visualizer, fixing the broken vertical striping. Also adds a table of morphological features to the visualization. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1581">#1581</a> Addresses <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/issues/1358">#1358</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Various small improvements to the web demo: route all responses to <code>/</code>; templatize <code>stanza-brat.html</code> so the version number is sourced from <code>_version.py</code>; move the logo to the demo directory for easier serving; add favicon support to the pipeline demo; guard against empty POST requests. <a href="https://redirect.github.com/stanfordnlp/stanza/pull/1582">#1582</a></p>
</li>
</ul>

<details>
<summary>Release notes</summary>
<p><em>Sourced from <a href="https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/python-sdk/releases">mcp's releases</a>.</em></p>
<blockquote>
<h2>v1.28.0</h2>
<h2>Deprecations</h2>
<p>Two API surfaces now emit <code>DeprecationWarning</code> ahead of their removal in v2. Nothing is removed in 1.x, and the warnings fire only when the deprecated API is <em>called</em> - importing the modules stays silent.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>WebSocket transport</strong> - <code>mcp.client.websocket.websocket_client</code> and <code>mcp.server.websocket.websocket_server</code><code>modelcontextprotocol/typescript-sdk#1783</code></li>
<li><strong>Experimental tasks API</strong> - <code>ClientSession.experimental</code>, <code>Server.experimental</code>, <code>ServerSession.experimental</code>, and the <code>experimental_task_handlers=</code> kwarg on <code>ClientSession</code>. Tasks (SEP-1686) were removed from the MCP specification and are expected to return as a separate MCP extension.</li>
</ul>
<p>If your test suite runs with <code>filterwarnings = [&quot;error&quot;]</code> and exercises these paths, add a scoped ignore such as <code>ignore:The experimental tasks API is deprecated:DeprecationWarning</code> or <code>ignore:The WebSocket .* transport is deprecated:DeprecationWarning</code>.</p>
<p>See <a href="https://redirect.github.com/modelcontextprotocol/python-sdk/issues/2828">#2828</a> for full details.</p>

</details>93:Ta77,## What
#16192's squash (8549adfc252) appended `DEEPGRAM_API_KEY` and `CARTESIA_API_KEY` to the **Publish Worker AI secrets** env block, but both keys were already defined ~30 lines up in the same block (added by #16525 / #16491 with routing-toggle comments). GitHub Actions refuses to even parse a workflow with duplicate env keys.

## Impact
Every cloud-cf-deploy trigger since the merge fails at the parse stage, before any job starts:
- push run [29602250875](https://github.com/elizaOS/eliza/actions/runs/29602250875) concluded `failure` with **zero jobs**
- `workflow_dispatch` returns HTTP 422: `'DEEPGRAM_API_KEY' is already defined, 'CARTESIA_API_KEY' is already defined`

Staging is frozen at `70dceb852` and cannot pick up develop (including the #16192 realtime voice client + staging flag it was meant to ship) until this parses again.

## Fix
Delete the two duplicate lines; the earlier definitions have identical values (`${{ secrets.* }}`). 2 deletions, no behavior change.

## Verification
- `bun test packages/scripts/__tests__/cloud-cf-voice-deploy-workflow.test.ts` -> 9/9 pass (the fail-closed realtime deploy contract, including the secret-gating + preflight bash extraction, still holds)
- YAML parses cleanly (`yaml.safe_load` OK); scanned every `env:` block in the workflow for duplicate keys -> none remain
